- **sheets-all**: Exécute la Phase 2 du prompt général (`CLAUDE-CODE-PROMPT.md`) : toutes les parties, dans l'ordre `01 02 03 08 06 05 07 04 00 09 B`, un sous-agent à contexte frais par spec `ready`, validateur après chaque retour, un commit par sheet, `tools/build_index.py` + push après chaque partie. Aucune question. Termine par le rapport de la Phase 3. ([chadlis/learnings](https://github.com/chadlis/learnings/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sheets-all.md))
- **sheets-batch**: Produis en série toutes les sheets dont le spec est `ready` dans la partie `$ARGUMENTS` (ex. `01`, `02`, `03`, `B`, `09`), une par sous-agent à contexte frais, dans l'ordre des numéros. ([chadlis/learnings](https://github.com/chadlis/learnings/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sheets-batch.md))
- **sheets-cell**: The detail view for **one cell**: what was entered, what it evaluates to, and — when it is a formula — the current values of every cell it references. `sheets read` is the list view; this is where an agent goes to find out *why* `D2` shows `#VALUE!`, or to confirm what a formula it wrote is actually computing from. One call, no scope beyond `spreadsheets`, read-only ([read-only-stays-read-only](.. ([JarvusInnovations/gws-axi](https://github.com/JarvusInnovations/gws-axi/blob/HEAD/specs/commands/sheets-cell.md) · ★10)
- **sheets-dimensions**: Structural edits that **shift data in place**: insert empty rows or columns at a position (everything below or to the right moves), or delete rows or columns (everything after them moves up or left). `sheets append` only ever adds rows at the end and `sheets clear` empties cells without moving anything; these four are the verbs for "make room in the middle" and "take this row out". Formulas elsewh ([JarvusInnovations/gws-axi](https://github.com/JarvusInnovations/gws-axi/blob/HEAD/specs/commands/sheets-dimensions.md) · ★10)
- **sheets-help**: Show how to connect Claude Code to a Google Sheet ([Vorski-Imagineering/METIS-pub](https://github.com/Vorski-Imagineering/METIS-pub/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sheets-help.md) · ★2)
- **sheets-note**: Set, replace or clear the **note** on one cell — the per-cell annotation `sheets read` lists in `notes[N]{cell,note}` and `sheets cell` shows as `note`. Notes are where a human or an agent leaves a "why" beside a value (`source: Q3 invoice`, `estimate — confirm with vendor`); until now gws-axi could read them but not write them. Distinct from **comments**, which are Drive comment threads (`sheets ([JarvusInnovations/gws-axi](https://github.com/JarvusInnovations/gws-axi/blob/HEAD/specs/commands/sheets-note.md) · ★10)
- **sheets-query**: Run a SQL-like query against one tab and get the result set — without writing a `=QUERY()` formula into the sheet. The language is Google's Visualization Query Language, the same one the `QUERY` function evaluates: `select`, `where`, `group by`, `pivot`, `order by`, `limit`, `offset`, `label`, `format`, with the aggregates `sum`, `avg`, `count`, `min`, `max` and the scalar functions for dates, str ([JarvusInnovations/gws-axi](https://github.com/JarvusInnovations/gws-axi/blob/HEAD/specs/commands/sheets-query.md) · ★10)
- **sheets-read**: Reads a native Google Sheets spreadsheet and renders one tab's cell values into an agent's context in a single pass. A spreadsheet's individual sheets *are* its tabs, so `sheets read` mirrors [`docs read`](docs-read.md)'s tab model exactly: it always lists the tabs, auto-renders the grid when there is only one (or when `--tab` picks one), and — when there are several and no `--tab` — returns just ([JarvusInnovations/gws-axi](https://github.com/JarvusInnovations/gws-axi/blob/HEAD/specs/commands/sheets-read.md) · ★10)

- **shepherd**: Shepherd one or an ordered sequence of open PRs all the way to merge — drain every review thread, pass CI, run the test plan, then auto-merge on a green gate. - **shepherd**: Autonomous tick loop that drives a whole GitHub Epic to completion. Snapshots open sub-issues, dispatches the next ready issue's implementer (rfc-loop / autopilot / inline), hands the resulting PR to pr-shepherd to drain reviews + merge, then regenerates the snapshot and recurses. Pair with /loop for unattended self-pacing. Hard stops (human CHANGES_REQUESTED, branch protection, conflict, ping-pong cap, implementer failure) pause the loop and surface the reason; operator resumes via `state.sh resume &lt;epic&gt;` after fixing the cause. ([enkira-ai/claude-plugins](https://github.com/enkira-ai/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/epic-shepherd/commands/shepherd.md) · ★2)
